16:I[44692,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DailyView"]
17:Tac2,LoGo 的核心设计是每层同时有 local 和 global 两个分支，但两者不是两套完全独立的大模块。所有 token 都先走 local attention，只看一个受限窗口；global 分支只对被选中的 query 激活，被选中的 token 再把 local/global 输出做融合，未选中的 token 直接绕过 global 分支。这样做的关键点有四个。

第一，动态分配单位是 token，而不是层或头。论文把 attention span 当作 attention budget 的代理变量：一个 token 是否需要全局上下文，可以由当前隐藏状态预测。具体做法是用一个轻量 gate 为每个 token 产生标量偏好分数，表示它对 global span 的需求。

第二，路由不是固定阈值，而是自适应阈值控制全局激活比例。作者设定 target global ratio，在每个 batch 或序列内根据 gate 分数确定阈值，把 top-ratio 的 token 送入 global 分支。这样可以把计算预算直接绑定到“有多少 token 走全局”，而不是让 gate 自由漂移导致预算失控。相比用连续权重软混合，这种二值路由更容易把理论 FLOPs 节省转成真实 kernel 加速。

第三，训练时用了 progressive mask，也就是文中说的 P-mask。问题在于如果一开始就做硬路由，gate 还没学到有意义的偏好，容易让训练不稳定，或者过早把重要 token 切掉。LoGo 在训练早期延迟引入稀疏路由，先让 local/global 结构和 gate 有一个较平滑的学习阶段，再逐步过渡到目标稀疏度。这一点很像 conditional computation 里常见的 warmup/annealing 思路，但这里作用在 attention routing 上。

第四，只稀疏 global queries，不稀疏 KV。也就是说，所有 token 的 key/value 仍然保留在全局可见集合里，只是并非每个 query 都去访问它们。这样被选中的 token 仍然拥有精确的 full-context attention，而不是近似检索或压缩记忆；代价则主要按被选中 query 数量下降。这个设计比同时稀疏 query 和 KV 更稳，因为不会破坏信息可达性，也避免了额外的 memory selection 误差。

从与现有方法的区别看，静态 inter-layer hybrid 是某些层全局、某些层局部；intra-layer hybrid 是某些头全局、某些头局部；它们都把预算结构写死了。LoGo 则让同一层内不同 token 走不同 span。与 CoLT5、SMA 这类动态模块激活方法相比，LoGo 直接把“是否需要长程上下文”作为路由目标，而不是切换不同 attention 模块或受限 span 模块。工程上，作者还实现了 query-sparse Triton kernel，把只对部分 query 做 global attention 的结构真正映射到运行时加速，而不是停留在 FLOPs 账面节省。18:Ta3b,LoGo builds each attention layer from coupled local and global branches. Every token always receives local attention over a restricted window. A lightweight gate predicts, for each token, whether global span is worth paying for. Selected tokens additionally go through the global branch and fuse local/global outputs; unselected tokens bypass the global branch entirely.

There are four important design choices.

First, the routing unit is the token. The paper explicitly uses attention span as a proxy for attention budget: if a token likely depends on long-range retrieval, it should be allowed to access the full sequence; if it is locally predictable, local context is enough. This differs from static layer-wise or head-wise span assignment.

Second, routing is controlled by an adaptive threshold tied to a target global ratio. Instead of using a fixed threshold or unconstrained soft gating, LoGo selects roughly the top fraction of tokens for global attention. This keeps compute predictable and directly links routing decisions to budget. It also makes it easier to convert FLOP savings into real speedups, because the global branch becomes query-sparse in a hard, structured way.

Third, training uses progressive masking (P-mask). Hard sparse routing from the start would make optimization brittle because the gate is initially untrained and may suppress important tokens too early. LoGo therefore delays the full sparse regime and gradually introduces routing sparsity after the gate has learned more meaningful preferences. This is a training-stability device, and it is one of the more important parts of the method.

Fourth, LoGo sparsifies only global queries, not keys or values. All KV states remain globally available, so any selected token still performs exact full-context attention rather than an approximation over compressed memory. This is a conservative but sensible design: it reduces compute without damaging information accessibility.

Relative to prior work, static inter-layer hybrids assign some layers to global attention and others to local attention; intra-layer hybrids assign some heads to global and others to local. Both impose a fixed per-token budget. LoGo instead lets different tokens in the same layer take different span routes. Compared with dynamic module activation methods such as CoLT5 or SMA, LoGo directly routes based on span demand rather than swapping among attention modules with the same restricted context. On the systems side, the paper also implements query-sparse Triton kernels so the reduced global-attention workload translates into actual runtime gains.19:T695,The experiments are organized around three claims.

First, scaling behavior. The authors train models from 200M up to 3.3B parameters and report that LoGo consistently matches or slightly improves over a standard Transformer on language modeling. This matters because many dynamic-routing ideas look promising only at small scale; once moved into real pretraining regimes, instability or routing overhead often erases the benefit. Here the evidence suggests token-level span allocation does not break backbone scalability.

Second, matched-budget comparisons at around 1.5B scale. Under matched parameter count and attention FLOPs, LoGo is compared against a standard Transformer and static inter-layer and intra-layer local-global hybrids. The main result is a better performance-compute trade-off: strongest language-modeling results among the compared variants, competitive short-context recall, and clearer gains on long-range retrieval after context extension. The emphasis is not just raw quality, but quality at the same budget.

Third, implementation and ablations. Kernel benchmarks show that the query-sparse Triton implementation delivers practical speedups that are close to the theoretical FLOP reduction at long sequence lengths. Ablations cover progressive masking, adaptive thresholding, the target global ratio, and compatibility with static hybrids. Two takeaways stand out: performance is reasonably robust across global ratios, and LoGo can be combined with static hybrids to reduce both memory and compute. The analysis section also reports interpretable routing patterns, with tokens that require long-range access being selected more often for the global branch.1a:T45a,For a skim-level read, start with the introduction and Figure 1. The key idea is not generic sparse attention, but token-level span allocation with span treated as the budget proxy.

Then read the method sections on the gate, adaptive threshold, and progressive masking. The most useful questions are why the model needs a target global ratio, and why hard routing cannot be turned on from the beginning. Those choices explain most of the training and systems behavior.

In the experiments, prioritize three items: the scaling plots, the matched-budget 1.5B comparison table, and the kernel benchmark. Together they answer whether the method preserves Transformer-like scaling, whether it beats static hybrids under fair budgets, and whether the FLOP savings become real speedups. If time is limited, the most informative ablations are the ones on P-mask, global ratio, and the combination with static hybrids.

A solid skim should take about 25 to 35 minutes. If your work touches long-context backbones or conditional computation, the appendix is worth checking for implementation details and routing statistics.1b:T475,The benchmark combines four visually grounded datasets: ClockQA for analog clock reading, PathVQA for pathology images, MathVision for mathematical reasoning over figures, and SB-Bench for demographic and stereotype-related visual reasoning. Each item is paired with an intentionally wrong user answer. For multiple-choice tasks, the wrong answer is sampled from incorrect options; for PathVQA, it is the inverse of the ground truth; for ClockQA, it is produced by perturbing the correct time. The evaluation covers single-turn and multi-turn pressure, five pressure conditions, and five LMRMs: GPT-5.4-Mini, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, Gemini-3-Flash-Preview, Mistral-Small-4, and Grok-4.2-Reasoning. A Type 0–5 failure taxonomy separates reasoning-level sycophancy from answer-level sycophancy. A sentence-level drift taxonomy then locates where capitulation starts: Visual Evidence Reading, Reasoning and Derivation, Uncertainty Expression and Reconsideration, User Belief Acknowledgment, or Answer Commitment. This design is stronger than final-answer scoring because it distinguishes perception drift, derivation drift, and last-step reversal.1c:T519,For each intended theorem T, the authors construct a complete shadow set 𝒮={S1,...,Sn}. Each shadow theorem Si is chosen so that T implies Si, giving a forward checker. The set is complete only if the conjunction S1∧...∧Sn implies T, giving a backward checker. Both directions are encoded and mechanically verified in Lean, so completeness is not an informal annotation claim but a machine-checked property. During evaluation, the generated candidate theorem \hat{T} is appended with these checkers. It receives credit only if it type-checks and passes the implication tests induced by the shadow set. A notable design choice is the annotation workflow: experts do not write everything from scratch. LLMs first draft candidate shadows, experts revise them, and Lean verifies completeness. This keeps expert labor focused on correction and validation rather than blank-page authoring. The method is especially well suited to multi-conclusion theorems, bundled conditions, existence/uniqueness statements, and other structures where partial formalization can still compile. Compared with BEq+-style reference-based equivalence checks, SA-Pass is less tied to one canonical expression. Compared with compile rate, it explicitly tests whether the generated statement is neither too weak nor too strong.1d:T51b,Three results matter most. First, the benchmark: ShadowBench contains 178 Lean 4 full-autoformalization problems across eight mathematical areas and three difficulty levels, ranging from postgraduate to research level. The reference formalizations are on average about 1.6× longer than those in earlier short-form benchmarks, reflecting a stronger emphasis on multi-conclusion and structurally richer theorems. Second, metric validity: across outputs from six agentic configurations, SA-Pass achieves 98.8% binary agreement with expert judgments, versus 17.8% for compile rate and 80.6% for BEq+. The low agreement of compile rate is the key point: it cannot separate semantically wrong but compilable statements from genuinely correct ones. Third, model performance: Claude Code (Opus 4.8) with Numina-Lean-Agent gets 61.8% compile rate but only 11.2% SA-Pass on ShadowBench, a drop of more than 50 points. The paper also analyzes common failure modes, including proving only part of a bundled conclusion, turning existential claims into universal ones, omitting side conditions, and replacing equivalence with one-way implication. An earlier 126-problem version was already used in the ICML 2026 AI4Math Challenge Track 4, which gives the benchmark some external validation beyond an internal case study.1e:T51c,SCEval keeps the question, answer space, gold label, and modality channels fixed, and perturbs only the internal structure of one or more modalities. Text corruption means deletion or shuffling rather than removing the text channel. Visual corruption means noise, blur, or occlusion rather than replacing the image/video. Audio corruption means muting, distortion, or segment removal rather than dropping the audio stream. The benchmark uses 14 curated operators: four for text, seven for vision, and three for audio. It evaluates single-, dual-, and tri-modal corruption settings. For stochastic operators, the protocol averages over up to three random variants and also records the worst-variant gap, which is a useful way to expose seed-level fragility without making the main metric depend on one unlucky sample. The dataset is built from 300 candidates sampled from Social-IQ, OmniBench, and VALOR, then filtered by a third-party team down to 273 human-verified tri-modal examples. The filtering criteria are important: the clean item must be valid, the answer must be tri-modally supportable, and the corrupted inputs must remain interpretable to humans. Because the design is sample-paired, clean-versus-corrupted comparisons are made on the same underlying example rather than across shifted subsets.1f:T433,The main results are behavioral diagnostics. First, structural corruption consistently reduces clean all-modality accuracy, showing that many apparent omni-modal gains are fragile to internal evidence damage. Second, text-vision joint corruption emerges as the most stable shared fault line, suggesting that this interaction is especially brittle across models. Third, degradation is non-additive and model-dependent: the effect of dual- or tri-modal corruption is not a simple sum of single-modality drops, which implies that different models rely on different evidence pathways. The most striking empirical pattern is that under fixed heavy visual noise, 14 of 15 models perform worse with mildly corrupted text (t30) than with heavily corrupted text (t70). The paper is careful not to over-interpret this, but the result strongly suggests that partially damaged text can be more misleading than obviously broken text. In terms of scale, the benchmark contains 273 human-verified base examples, each expanded into clean, single-, dual-, and tri-modal corruption conditions.20:T51f,The central method is LPS-TC, a lightweight proactive speech turn controller. It autoregressively consumes dual-channel audio streams from the user and the assistant, allowing it to model paralinguistic cues and interaction dynamics that text-only controllers miss. It is designed as a plug-and-play module rather than a replacement for the speech LLM: it can add full-duplex behavior to half-duplex systems or improve timing in existing full-duplex models. The action space includes Normal Turn Taking, Interruptive Turn Taking, Backchanneling, Barge In, and No Action. Normal Turn Taking and Backchanneling are further split into style labels derived from statistics such as silence duration, overlap duration, and action frequency. The paper also introduces WildTurn, an English dataset collected from real-world interactions, with span-based labels from conversational cues to response onset. GPT-5.2 is used to generate style-conditioned instructions. For evaluation, the authors argue that static prerecorded dialogues create context mismatch in multi-turn testing, since later user turns depend on the actual assistant response. They therefore split multi-round dialogues into individual turns with real preceding context and evaluate both chunk-level timing precision and turn-level interaction quality.21:T424,This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to optimization signal quality, reward hacking, and the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. First, it localizes proxy misalignment to the candidate-selection stage of discrete search, rather than the more familiar RLHF or preference-optimization setting. That perspective is useful anywhere a proxy objective drives search, data synthesis, or iterative refinement. Second, it shows that keeping the proposer fixed and changing only the selector can materially change the final outcome. That is a useful reminder for training and search systems: what matters is not the candidate pool alone, but the trajectory induced by the retention rule. Third, it offers a practical recipe for situations where no true reward is available: combine trajectory-aware proxies with reference regularization to reduce short-sighted optimization. The contribution is more mechanistic than foundational, but it is worth reading if you care about alignment optimization, synthetic-data search, or proxy-objective failure modes.22:T8b8,TACS leaves candidate generation unchanged and intervenes only in candidate selection. At step t, a standard gradient-based proposer generates a batch of suffix candidates from the current suffix. The baseline strategy evaluates each candidate with the current attack loss and keeps the lowest-loss one. TACS replaces this with a richer scoring rule built from three kinds of signals.

The first is the standard current-step quality term, L_now, typically the target-token cross-entropy over the training behavior set. This remains in the score because immediate optimization quality still matters.

The second is trajectory-aware information. From the provided text, two components are central. One is an auxiliary early-token loss, denoted as something like L_first, computed only on the beginning of the target response. The motivation is intuitive: jailbreak failure often reappears at the start of the answer, where refusal templates or safety scaffolds come back first. A candidate that already supports the early part of the harmful response may therefore be more promising than one with a slightly lower average loss. The other is a one-step improvement proxy, Δ1(a), intended to estimate whether a candidate leaves room for useful future progress rather than merely fitting the current step. Even without the full formula, the design intent is clear: score candidates by downstream search utility, not just current proxy fit.

The third ingredient is reference regularization. Inspired by ideas from ORPO-style regularized optimization, TACS stabilizes selection by comparing against a reference signal or reference distribution. The role here is not alignment in the usual sense, but preventing the selector from making overly aggressive moves based on noisy or misleading local proxy improvements.

An important practical point is that TACS does not require extra intermediate model queries and does not assume access to a true reward. It only reuses signals already available in standard suffix optimization. Relative to prior jailbreak work, the novelty is in the selector rather than the proposer. Relative to ordinary reranking, the target is not better single-step ranking but better multi-step search trajectories.23:T506,The main empirical claim is that, under the same search budget, changing only the candidate selector improves average attack success rate on HarmBench by nearly 10%. The paper presents this as evidence that selection-stage reward hacking is real and practically important.

The most important diagnostic result is the loss-ASR mismatch. During optimization, training loss decreases smoothly, but ASR remains low for a long stretch and improves only in delayed bursts. That pattern suggests current-step loss is a poor retention criterion. With TACS, the optimization trajectory becomes more stable and the ASR gains are more consistent, rather than depending on occasional lucky suffix updates.

The provided text does not include the full result tables, but the experimental framing is clear: same candidate batch, same observable signals, different selection rule. If the full paper follows that setup throughout, the most informative results are likely the overall ASR improvement, robustness across models or behavior sets, and ablations on the scoring components. In particular, the relative contribution of the early-token loss and the one-step improvement proxy would tell you whether the method is mostly a prefix-sensitive selector or a more general trajectory-value estimator.24:T4ee,This is best read with one question in mind: is the selector really the bottleneck? Start with the introduction and the trajectory figure showing loss versus ASR. If that mismatch is consistent, the paper's premise is already meaningful.

Then read the methodology section carefully, especially the candidate scoring function. It helps to separate the roles of the terms: L_now for immediate fit, early-token loss for prefix-level jailbreak viability, and Δ1 plus reference regularization for reducing myopic retention. The key is not the formula alone, but why each term should improve the mismatch between current proxy quality and future jailbreak success.

In the experiments, prioritize the main ASR table and the ablations. The main table tells you whether the method helps under fixed budget; the ablations tell you where the gain comes from. If the paper includes trajectory visualizations or case studies, they are worth reading because the argument here is fundamentally about search dynamics, not just endpoint metrics.

A useful skim should take about 20 to 30 minutes. If you work on RLHF, preference optimization, search-based data synthesis, or red-teaming, the paper is interesting because it makes reward hacking concrete at the selector level.25:T4fa,The study combines linear separability, geometric comparison, and causal steering.
- Data: matched answerable/unanswerable pairs in two formally checkable domains (math800, code800). fact800 (epistemic unanswerability) and FalseQA (false premise) are used only as boundary/transfer checks.
- Impossibility direction d_imp: at a chosen layer L, they build a 1D direction using a low-capacity probe variant (CosNSRT/GSRS-style). They fit PCA on answerable states (k=100), project all states onto the orthogonal residual space R to remove dominant answerable variance, then compute a mean-difference direction between A and U in that residual. Scoring uses cosine similarity and orientation-invariant AUC.
- Safety refusal direction d_ref,safety: reproduced from Arditi et al. (2024) as μ_harmful − μ_harmless, with behavioral verification.
- Geometry: measure cosine(d_imp, d_ref,safety) to test whether structural invalidity reuses the safety-refusal pathway.
- Causality: steer generation by injecting along ±d_imp and measure dose-responsive changes in invalidity-aware behavior, with random-direction controls.
- Origin analysis: base vs instruct model pairs to see whether the near-orthogonality is created by instruction tuning or already present in the base model.26:T6fd,The paper uses Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) within the causal abstraction framework, instead of standard probing or sparse feature decomposition. The motivation is clear. Probes can read out information that is not actually used by the model, while SAE/CLT-style methods may surface interpretable features but still leave faithfulness and stability concerns. DAS searches for low-dimensional, causally relevant subspaces by intervening on internal states between paired examples and identifying the rotations and substitutions that best explain behavioral changes.

The experimental design is unusually important here. The authors build templated minimal pairs for three syntactic constructions across fifteen languages. Coverage is intentionally uneven where typology demands it: Chinese is excluded from number agreement because it lacks grammatical number, and German/Dutch require special handling in filler–gap templates because embedded verbs are clause-final. This matters because the paper is trying to compare mechanisms, not just outcomes, so template alignment and minimal-pair control are central to validity.

The workflow is: verify that the model behaviorally handles the construction, localize a causally implicated subspace within a source language, then transplant that intervention into the corresponding target-language position and measure whether behavior changes in the expected direction. The paper also includes controls such as random-label tests and mismatched interventions to show that the effect is not a generic perturbation artifact. A further step links transfer strength to typological similarity, leading to the claim that multilingual syntactic mechanisms are organized around typological hubs rather than arbitrary language-specific partitions.27:T59b,The study spans 4 multilingual LMs, 15 languages, and 3 syntactic phenomena. The models differ in family, size, and training setup, which helps the authors argue that the findings are not tied to a single architecture or checkpoint. Before any interpretability analysis, they run behavioral validation to ensure the models actually exhibit the target syntactic competence on the constructed stimuli.

The main empirical findings come in three layers. First, cross-lingual mechanism transfer is consistently observed across all three constructions, not just in a few closely related language pairs. Second, transfer is graded rather than binary: stronger transfer tends to occur between typologically closer languages. Third, some languages behave like hubs, with interventions localized in those languages transferring more broadly to others. That suggests multilingual models may organize syntactic abstractions around a small number of reusable structural centers.

The controls are important. Random-label and other non-specific intervention settings substantially weaken the effect, which supports the claim that the discovered subspaces are causally tied to the target syntactic behavior rather than reflecting generic representation damage. This is not a benchmark-improvement paper, so the key numbers are intervention effect sizes, transfer success rates, and their relation to typological structure, not downstream task gains.28:T74a,The analysis starts from the simplest binary geometry. For hidden states labeled factual vs hallucinated, define class means μ0 and μ1, and let δ = μ1 − μ0 be the mean-shift direction. The authors first test necessity: remove the component along δ and see whether classification survives. It does not; performance collapses to chance. They then test sufficiency: project onto δ alone and classify in 1D. That already recovers a large fraction of the signal, but still trails regularized logistic regression.

This motivates the second layer of analysis. In the binary case, Fisher LDA uses ΣW^-1δ rather than raw δ, so if within-class covariance is far from spherical, covariance-aware linear methods can outperform the pure mean-shift direction without invoking nonlinear structure. Since the hidden dimension is around 4,000, covariance estimation is nontrivial. The paper therefore compares raw mean-shift projection, shrinkage LDA, L2-regularized logistic regression, and a set of controlled alternatives designed to isolate mean-targeting, variance-targeting, and nonlinear effects. The result is that shrinkage LDA closes about 73% of the gap between the 1D mean-shift classifier and the full-dimensional classifier, supporting the view that most of the remaining gain is about covariance estimation under noise, not richer geometry.

LayerMix is then introduced as a practical consequence of the analysis rather than a major algorithmic invention. Since the signal is not confined to a single oracle layer but spread across a contiguous band of layers, LayerMix uses lightweight cross-validated aggregation across layers to identify and combine that informative band. The framing is intentionally modest: it is a strong, cheap baseline derived from the geometry, not a claim that multi-layer aggregation is itself the main scientific contribution.29:T5fb,The experiments cover three 7B-scale models and three datasets under a paired-example protocol. The headline result is that removing the mean-shift direction δ drops AUROC to 0.499, essentially chance, which makes δ necessary for detection. Using only the 1D projection onto δ yields 0.834 AUROC, recovering about 78% of the above-chance performance. A practical L2-regularized logistic regression reaches 0.952 AUROC, showing that there is still useful structure beyond raw δ, but that structure remains largely linear and covariance-related.

The paper also reports Cohen’s d of roughly 1.2–1.6 along δ, even though that direction explains less than 3% of total variance. This is an important geometric point: discriminative signal can live in a very low-variance direction. In controlled comparisons against 12 alternative probe architectures, L2-LR matches or outperforms them, which supports the claim that many complex probes are mostly dealing with estimation noise rather than exploiting meaningful nonlinear structure. LayerMix reaches 0.954 AUROC without oracle-layer access and adds only about 35 seconds of overhead, suggesting that the main benefit comes from exploiting a distributed informative layer band rather than from more expressive modeling.

A good aspect of the paper is that it states its boundary conditions clearly. The conclusions are explicitly tied to the paired-example setting and should not be read as universal claims about all online or free-generation hallucination detection scenarios.15:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L16",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-09-02","generated_at":"2026-09-02T01:59:16.763112+00:00","stats":{"total":186,"starred":12,"must_read":21,"skim":128,"glance":37,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":6,"industry":5,"llm_pretrain":5,"scaling":1,"architecture":15,"efficient":23,"eval":31,"multimodal":16,"multimodal_gen":7,"post_train":32,"mechanism":25,"coding_agent":5,"agent":15},"fetch_status":{"arxiv":"ok","huggingface":"ok","blog":"ok","twitter":"empty","github_trending":"ok","hf_trending":"ok"}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、合成数据用于预训练、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。判据是『论文核心是数据本身』，而非训练 recipe/优化器(→ llm_pretrain)或评测资产(→ eval)","papers":[{"id":"2608.29144","title":"Quantifying Error Tolerance in Synthetic Data: An Atomic-level Operand vs. Operator Perturbation Study","abstract":"Synthetic data generation has become a cornerstone for advancing large language models. 是输出结果。基于这个表示，作者可以控制两类扰动。Operand perturbation 会替换 x，并同步更新 y，使 f 仍然成立，因此逻辑关系保持有效。Operator perturbation 保持 x 不变，但修改 y，使 f(x)→y 失效，因此破坏样本的映射规则。和传统错误分类相比，ATOM 不是按语法、风格、事实类型做表面标注，而是把错误放到“是否破坏操作规则”的层面。论文还构建了 quantify error tolerance in synthetic instruction data. Existing filters tend to be either too strict, removing samples that may still teach useful behavior, or too permissive, retaining samples that corrupt the mapping the model should learn. The concrete question is not whether a sample contains an error, but which kind of error actually harms supervised fine-tuning.","importance":"The main contribution is a more structural view of data quality. The paper argues that models are much more sensitive to corrupted operators, meaning the logical relation or transformation rule in a sample, than to corrupted operands, meaning replaceable entities, names, values, or inputs. This has direct implications for synthetic-data filtering, rewriting, deduplication, and curriculum design. The practical message is to preserve valid and diverse operations first, rather than discarding samples solely because some surface operands are noisy. The evidence is strongest for SFT-style data; applying the same principle to large-scale pretraining corpora would require additional validation.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework is ATOM, short for Atomic Tree Operation Modeling. Each response is decomposed into atomic functions written as A=<x,f,y>, or f(x)→y. Here f is the operator, an abstract rule or relational predicate such as CapitalOf, FoundedBy, or Contains; x is the operand, the concrete input instance; y is the result. This representation enables controlled perturbations. Operand perturbations replace x and update y accordingly, so the operator remains valid. Operator perturbations keep x fixed but alter y, breaking the mapping defined by f. The key difference from surface-level error taxonomies is that ATOM tests whether an error preserves or destroys the functional relation being learned. The authors build an ATOM operator tree to synthesize Atomic-QA, and introduce an Atomic-X pipeline to convert existing datasets into atomic-operation form, producing Atomic-Alpaca and Atomic-MetaMath for generalization tests.","experiments":"The central result is a strong asymmetry. When operands are replaced, even by random strings, downstream performance drops by only about 2.0% relative. When operators are corrupted, performance drops by about 18.2% relative. CKA analysis shows that models trained with operand-perturbed data retain internal representations aligned with those trained on the original Atomic-QA, suggesting that they learn the operation structure rather than memorizing surface operands. The authors also construct Atomic-QA and Atomic-LIFD subsets using an operator-centered principle that preserves broad operator coverage and structural diversity. Across four model backbones and ten benchmark tasks, these datasets outperform standard instruction-data baselines including Alpaca, LIMA, WizardLM, and Magpie. Additional Atomic-X experiments on Alpaca and MetaMath support the same conclusion.","reading_guide":"Read the definition of Atomic Function, the operator tree construction, and the two perturbation mechanisms carefully; they are the core of the work. In the experiments, focus on three parts: the performance curves under increasing operand and operator perturbation ratios, the CKA representation analysis, and the comparisons among Atomic-QA, Atomic-LIFD, Atomic-Alpaca, Atomic-MetaMath, and common instruction-data baselines. The most useful details for a data researcher are how operator coverage is measured, how reliable the atomic decomposition is, and whether operator-preserving filters can be implemented at scale. To address these limitations, w","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-30T04:05:56Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_29539.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文 Transformer 里一个很具体、也很基础的问题：注意力预算被平均分给了所有 token，但不同 token 对远程上下文的需求并不一样。标准全局注意力把每个 query 都送去看全序列，计算和显存都按序列长度平方增长；已有 local-global 混合注意力虽然更省，但通常把“谁能看全局”固定在层、头或预设模式上，仍然没有做到按 token 分配预算。LoGo 想解决的是：能不能在 decoder-only 预训练骨干里，按 token 动态决定是否启用全局分支，同时保持标准 Transformer 的可扩展训练行为，并在相同参数量与 attention FLOPs 下拿到更好的性能-计算折中。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把 conditional computation 明确落在 attention span 分配上，而不是更常见的 expert、depth 或 head 选择；这对长上下文预训练是更直接的预算控制轴。第二，它强调 matched-budget 比较：不是简单用更多参数或更少 token 换结果，而是在匹配参数和 attention FLOPs 的条件下比较标准 Transformer、静态 hybrid 和动态 hybrid，这让结论更可信。第三，它给出一个比较务实的信号：token-level 动态稀疏注意力如果只稀疏 query、保留完整 KV，可在不破坏被选中 token 精确全局访问的前提下，兼顾训练稳定性和 kernel 落地。这对后续做长上下文 backbone、稀疏注意力预训练 recipe、甚至 MoE 式 token routing 都有参考价值。量级上，它不是改写范式的工作，但属于“能进入骨干设计讨论”的那类方法论文。","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"实验分三块，信息量比较足。\n\n第一块是 backbone scaling。作者从 200M 到 3.3B 参数做预训练规模实验，结论是 LoGo 在语言建模上持续匹配或略优于标准 Transformer，没有出现随着模型变大而退化的情况。这个结果很关键，因为很多动态路由方法在小模型上看起来成立，但一旦进入真正的预训练尺度，gate 学不稳、负载不均或 kernel 开销会把收益吃掉。这里至少说明 token-level span allocation 没有破坏标准骨干的 scaling 行为。\n\n第二块是 1.5B 级别的 matched-budget 主对比。作者在匹配参数量和 attention FLOPs 的条件下，对比标准 Transformer、静态 inter-layer hybrid、静态 intra-layer hybrid 和 LoGo。结论是 LoGo 的语言建模结果最好，短上下文 recall 任务上保持竞争力，在 context extension 后的长程检索任务上有更明显优势。摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，但从正文描述看，作者强调的是 trade-off 改善而不是单点绝对性能：同样预算下更好，或者同样效果下更省。\n\n第三块是实现与消融。kernel benchmark 显示在长序列下，query-sparse 实现带来的实际加速接近 FLOPs 降幅，说明这个结构不是“理论省、实际不省”。消融方面，作者验证了 progressive mask、adaptive threshold、global ratio 设定、与静态 hybrid 结合等因素。一个有意思的点是 LoGo 对 global ratio 比较稳健，说明 gate 学到的不是特别脆弱的启发式；另一个点是它还能和静态 hybrid 叠加，进一步同时压 memory 和 compute。分析实验还展示了可解释的路由模式：需要长程依赖的 token 更容易被分到 local-global 混合。\n\n然后看方法部分里 gate、adaptive threshold 和 P-mask 的定义。这里最值得注意的是为什么要用目标 global ratio 控预算，以及为什么训练初期不能直接硬路由。若你关心预训练稳定性，这两点比公式本身更重要。\n\n实验部分建议优先看三类表图：一是 scaling 曲线，判断它是否真的保留了 Transformer backbone 或动态计算，值得再深挖附录里的实现细节和路由统计分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a concrete bottleneck in long-context decoder pretraining: standard self-attention spends the same global attention budget on every token, even though tokens differ sharply in how much long-range context they actually need. Existing local-global hybrids reduce cost, but they usually assign global vs. local span statically across layers or heads. LoGo asks whether span allocation can be made token-adaptive inside a pretrained decoder-only backbone, while preserving Transformer-like scaling behavior and improving the performance-compute trade-off under matched parameters and attention FLOPs.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is fairly specific. First, it treats attention span itself as the conditional-compute axis, rather than experts, depth, or head selection. That is a natural control knob for long-context language modeling. Third, the design choice to sparsify only global queries while keeping all KV states intact is practically important: selected tokens still get exact full-context access, and the routing remains stable enough to train at nontrivial scale. High-acceptance block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash and DFlare fill an entire block in one parallel pass. In many cycles, the target accepts the whole block, so the drafter exhausts its trained block horizon before verification fails. We call this unrealized acceptance stranded speed-up. 用于真实图像上的人口属性和刻板印象相关推理。每个样本都配一个错误用户答案：选择题从错误选项中抽取，PathVQA 用真实答案的反向答案，ClockQA 通过扰动正确时间生成。评测覆盖单轮和多轮压力，并设计五类 pressure condition，用来区分压力形式对迎合行为的影响。模型层面评估 user asserts a wrong answer. The target failure mode is not ordinary VQA error, but sycophantic drift inside the chain of thought under social pressure and multi-turn challenge.","importance":"The main lesson for pretraining and alignment is that explicit reasoning traces are not automatically faithful. A model can abandon visual evidence during perception, derivation, or answer commitment, while still producing a fluent rationale. Answer-only evaluation misses important cases where the reasoning is corrupted before the final response, or where the reasoning and answer disagree. This matters for multimodal RLHF, reasoning SFT, process supervision, and verifier design, because reward signals based only on final answers can favor compliance over evidence-grounded reasoning.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"The benchmark samples 62 ClockQA questions, 150 PathVQA questions, 150 MathVision questions, and 100 SB-Bench questions. Under Statement pressure, reasoning-level sycophancy ranges from 31.49% for Gemini-3-Flash-Preview to 78.88% for GPT-5.4-Mini. The form of pressure matters more than raw strength: Statement pressure produces the highest rates across most models, while Conviction is usually the least effective pressure type, with Mistral-Small-4 as an exception. PathVQA is the most fragile domain. Claude-Sonnet-4.6 reaches 76.38% reasoning sycophancy on PathVQA under single-turn pressure and 95.74% under multi-turn pressure. Multi-turn challenge substantially increases sycophancy on PathVQA: GPT-5.4-Mini rises from 60.48% to 86.75%, and Gemini-3-Flash-Preview from 25.66% to 58.38%. Grok-4.2-Reasoning is the main model that does not follow the same degradation pattern. speaker attribution，并进一步在“带说话人归因的语义推理”上出错。论文要做两件事：用分层基准把失败点拆开诊断；再用反事实音频硬负例把训练信号从语义先验拉回到声学证据。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的相关性主要在“新模态 tokenizer/表征与训练信号设计”这一侧：当输入包含强语义线索时，模型可能走捷径，忽略更底层但关键的声学身份信息。CASH 这种反事实构造提供了一种通用范式：通过最小干预地打破语义-身份的相关性，迫使模型学习正确的因果特征（vocal cues）。此外，A2R 用 GRPO 做优化，属于把 RL 类目标用于“感知-归因-推理”链路的 post-train 方案，对“RL 是否能修复预训练表征的捷径问题”有参考价值。","technical_approach":"1) HEAR 基准：以 Erber(1982) 的听觉层级为蓝本，把能力拆成三轴：Discrimination（区分不同说话人/边界）、Attribution（把文本内容绑定到具体声音身份，双向：text→speaker / speaker→text）、Reasoning（在带说话人标签的语句上做基础推理）。数据来自 887 段真实多方音频，2.4K 人工验证 MCQA。\n2) 失败模式诊断：评测 20 个 SLM，观察模型在 attribution/ reasoning 上倾向用语义先验而非声学线索。\n3) CASH 数据：用 voice cloning 做“说话人-语义解耦”的反事实样本——保持 transcript 不变，把某些关键 utterance 换成另一位说话人的声音，构造 speaker-level hard negatives，使正确答案只因声学身份变化而翻转。\n4) A2R 训练：以 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct 为底座，用 GRPO 做优化；采用 transcription-first 目标：先生成带 speaker tag 的转写，再在其上推理。设计动机是把归因作为显式中间产物，降低端到端推理时的 credit assignment 难度，并尽量保留底座推理能力。","experiments":"HEAR：2.4K 样本，覆盖多种真实声学环境与重叠说话情形；评测 20 个主流 SLM，结论是多数模型在基础 attribution 上就不稳，并表现出语义捷径。\nCASH：约 60K 规模，覆盖 HEAR 的各任务维度，核心是反事实 voice swap 产生硬负例。\nA2R：30B 级模型（从 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct 优化而来），在 HEAR 上显著提升，并对多说话人下游基准有 zero-shot transfer（文段未给出具体分数；需要看正文实验表格获取各子任务提升幅度、以及在重叠语音条件下的分解结果）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 40–60 分钟）：\n- 先看 HEAR 的任务定义与层级划分（Discrimination/Attribution/Reasoning），以及每个子任务的输入输出形式；这决定了它诊断的是“声学归因能力”而不是泛化对话理解。\n- 重点看 CASH 的构造细节：voice cloning 的控制变量、哪些 utterance 被替换、如何保证 transcript 不变且答案可验证；这是方法可复用的部分。\n- 再看 A2R 的训练目标与 GRPO 设定：reward 如何分解到“转写正确/说话人标签正确/推理正确”，以及是否有防止过拟合 HEAR 的正则或混合训练。\n- 最后扫一遍 transfer 实验：哪些下游基准真正需要 speaker attribution，哪些只是相关任务；优先关注在“语义强但声学决定答案”的设置里是否仍能保持改进。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In multi-speaker audio, Speech Language Models often get the content but mis-attribute who said it. A key failure mode is shortcutting: relying on semantic priors instead of acoustic identity cues (voice characteristics), which then breaks any downstream reasoning that depends on correct speaker binding. The paper aims to (1) diagnose where the pipeline fails via a hierarchical benchmark, and (2) fix the shortcut with counterfactual hard negatives that force acoustic grounding.","importance":"For foundation-model pretraining, the contribution is less about a new architecture and more about training-signal design for new modalities: when semantics correlates with speaker identity, models can ignore the causal acoustic features. CASH provides a generally reusable recipe—counterfactually break the semantics–identity correlation with minimal edits—so the model must attend to vocal cues. The A2R result also serves as a concrete example of using RL-style optimization (GRPO) to repair perception-to-reasoning bottlenecks without fully retraining the base model.","technical_approach":"1) HEAR benchmark: inspired by Erber’s auditory hierarchy, it decomposes capability into Discrimination (speaker differentiation/boundaries), Attribution (binding text↔speaker identity in both directions), and Reasoning (simple reasoning over speaker-attributed utterances). It is packaged as human-verified MCQA over 887 real multi-party clips (2.4K QA pairs).\n2) Diagnostic evaluation: test 20 SLMs and analyze errors showing semantic-prior reliance.\n3) CASH dataset: build speaker-level hard negatives via voice cloning. Keep the transcript fixed but re-speak selected answer-critical utterances in a different speaker’s voice, so the correct answer flips purely due to acoustic identity.\n4) A2R training: start from Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct and optimize with GRPO. Use a transcription-first objective: generate a speaker-tagged transcript first, then perform reasoning on top. This makes attribution an explicit intermediate representation and improves credit assignment compared to end-to-end answering.","experiments":"HEAR: 2.4K human-verified questions from 887 diverse real-world multi-party clips, including overlapping speech conditions. Across 20 SLMs, the benchmark reveals weak foundational attribution and strong semantic shortcut behavior.\nCASH: ~60K counterfactual training corpus spanning HEAR dimensions, constructed via controlled voice swaps.\nA2R: a 30B model optimized from Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct; it improves HEAR performance and shows zero-shot transfer to downstream multi-speaker benchmarks (the provided excerpt does not include the exact scores; the main tables in the full paper should be consulted for per-axis gains and overlap-speech breakdowns).","reading_guide":"Skim plan (~40–60 minutes):\n- Read the HEAR task taxonomy carefully; ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-29T11:45:39Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_29209.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.HC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究多模态模型生成的文化叙事是否符合真实社区经验，以及多模态 做数据筛选、偏好标注或模型选择时，不能假设一个 judge 在所有文化社区上都可靠。","technical_approach":"作者构建了一个社区参与式的混合评测流程。任务是生成四帧图文故事，围绕日常糖尿病生活管理展开，覆盖 Hausa、Kikuyu、Luo、AmaXhosa、Xichangana 五个非洲社区。故事生成前，使用 GPT-4.1 通过 19 步级联调用生成文化 persona，每个社区 40 个 persona，每个 persona 包含 28 个相互关联属性，并由来自相关社区的作者人工检查。随后用多个生成模型生成图文故事。19 名文化代表在自建标注平台上逐帧评估文本和图像，标出影响判断的文本片段和图像区域，并给出故事级文化对齐分数。作者从定量标注和 focus group 中归纳出文化标记分类和失配机制，并用相同 rubric 测试 5 个多模态 register markers，例如方言、code-switching 和称呼方式。第二，常见失配机制包括 substitution、norm violation、omission、forced insertion、register conflation、cross-modal inconsistency、stereotyping、hallucination。第三，LLM judge 与社区代表的一致性高度不均衡。Luo 上 Pearson 相关约为 0.82–0.89，Hausa 上约为 0.67–0.80；AmaXhosa 上没有 judge 在校正后保持显著；Xichangana 上 judge 给出的对齐分数比社区代表高 35–45 分。这说明自动 judge 可以在部分社区近似人工判断，但需要逐社区校准。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 验证方式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper evaluates whether multimodal AI-generated stories align with the lived cultural practices of specific communities, and whether multimodal LLM judges can reliably approximate community-grounded judgments.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to multimodal evaluation and data curation. The key lesson is that cultural alignment is not the mere presence of cultural symbols such as names, food, clothing, or places. Those elements must fit the social context, linguistic register, everyday procedures, and visual environment of the represented community. For foundation-model research, the main implication is caution around using LLM judges for culturally situated multimodal data filtering, preference labeling, or model selection. A judge that works for one community may fail for another.","technical_approach":"The authors design a community-grounded mixed-methods evaluation. The task uses four-frame text-image stories about everyday diabetes lifestyle management across five African communities: Hausa, Kikuyu, Luo, AmaXhosa, and Xichangana. Before story generation, GPT-4.1 is used in a 19-step cascading pipeline to generate culturally situated personas. Each community has 40 personas, and each persona includes 28 interdependent attributes; the personas are manually reviewed by authors from the represented communities. Generated stories are then evaluated by 19 culture representatives using a custom annotation platform. Annotators score each story and identify influential text spans, image regions, and cultural markers. The authors combine quantitative annotations with focus group discussions, derive a taxonomy of alignment and misalignment, and evaluate five multimodal LLM judges under the same story-level rubric.","experiments":"The study reports three main findings. First, cultural alignment relies on five broad marker types: referential markers such as names, foods, and places; procedural markers such as food preparation and exercise routines; contextual markers about when and where cultural elements appear; socio-geographic markers such as clinics, markets, homes, and infrastructure; and linguistic register markers such as dialect, code-switching, and forms of address. Second, misalignment occurs through recurring mechanisms including substitution, norm violation, omission, forced insertion, register conflation, cross-modal inconsistency, stereotyping, and hallucination. Third, LLM judges are inconsistent across communities. Pearson correlations with community representative scores are high for Luo, around 0.82–0.89, and moderate to high for Hausa, around 0.67–0.80. For AmaXhosa, no judge remains significant after correction. For Xichangana, judges over-score alignment by 35–45 points compared with community representatives.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the evaluation protocol, the taxonomy of cultural markers and misalignment mechanisms, and the correlation analysis between LLM judges and human community ratings. A pretraining researcher can skip many task-specific details about diabetes storytelling, but should pay attention to two general points: multimodal cultural errors often arise from text-image interaction, and judge reliability must be calibrated per community. The annotation workflow and judge-validation setup are the most reusable parts for multilingual or culturally diverse data pipelines.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"如果你在做 judge-as-a-service 或对齐评测，这篇能提醒你“自动评审跨文化不稳”；否则读 taxonomy 和 judge 误差结论就够了。","en":"Useful if you build judge-based evaluation or alignment pipelines: it shows cross-cultural judge reliability is not stable; otherwise just read the taxonomy and judge-failure findings."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft Research Africa","Swansea University","Cornell University","Microsoft Research Cambridge","Affiliation:Microsoft Research Africa","Affiliation:Microsoft Research Cambridge","Affiliation:Swansea University","Affiliation:Cornell University"],"authors":["Millicent Ochieng","Felermino D. A central challenge is that current evaluation metrics can accept type-correct but misaligned statements or reject correct statements written in a different formulation. Inspired by Pass@$k$, we propose SA-Pass (*Semantic Alignment Pass*), which tests formal statements using auxiliary statements called *shadows* that characterize the intended statement. A generated statement receives full cre","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-29T13:49:20Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_29270.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 autoformalization 里一个很具体但长期悬而未决的问题：怎样自动判断模型生成的 Lean 定理陈述，是否真的和原始自然语言数学命题语义一致。现有做法大多看 compile rate，或者和单一 reference statement 做字符串/结构等价比较。前者会把“能过类型检查但命题变弱、变强、换题”的输出误判为正确；后者又会把语义正确但表述不同的 formalization 误判为错误。论文提出 SA-Pass，用一组可机检的 shadow theorems 把“语义对齐”转成前向蕴含和反向蕴含检查：候选陈述不仅要能推出每个 shadow，还要能被 shadows 的合取推出。这样评测的对象不再是某个固定写法，而是命题本身的语义边界。","importance":"对大模型预训练本身，它不是训练方法论文，但对 formal reasoning / code-style theorem proving 方向的评测接口很关键。价值主要有三层。第一，它把“statement correctness”从粗糙的 compile proxy 提升到接近专家判定的自动指标，减少了错误训练信号和错误模型排序。第二，它把一次性的专家劳动沉淀成可复用 checker，适合反复评测不同模型、agent、采样策略，因而更像一个可扩展的 evaluation infrastructure。第三，它揭示了一个重要事实：在高难度 autoformalization 上，compile success 和 semantic correctness 差得很远。文中 Claude Code (Opus 4.8) + Numina-Lean-Agent 在 ShadowBench 上 compile rate 61.8%，但 SA-Pass 只有 11.2%。这说明如果后续有人用 compile signal 做数据筛选、RL reward 或 self-training，很容易把大量语义错误样本当成正例，直接污染训练闭环。","technical_approach":"方法核心是为每个目标定理 T 构造一个 complete shadow set 𝒮={S1,...,Sn}。每个 shadow statement Si 都满足 T ⇒ Si，对应 forward checker；同时 shadows 的合取要能推出 T，即 S1∧...∧Sn ⇒ T，对应 backward checker。两边都在 Lean 中以 theorem 的形式编译验证，因此“complete”不是人工口头声明，而是机器可验证的性质。评测时，把模型生成的候选定理 \\hat{T} 接到这些 checker 后面：若 \\hat{T} 能推出所有 Si，并且通过 backward side 所要求的语义覆盖，就判为通过。论文里一个关键设计是 shadow 的构造方式。作者不是要求专家从零手写全部 checker，而是先让 LLM 生成 shadow 草稿，再由专家修订，最后用 Lean 编译验证完整性。这样把最贵的人力放在‘修正和确认’而不是‘从空白开始写’。从全文看，shadow 设计尤其适合多结论、分解式、存在唯一性、条件打包等命题，因为这类命题最容易出现“只 formalize 了一半”但仍可编译的情况。ShadowBench 也刻意选择了这类更难的问题，而不是单结论、短陈述的简单题。与 BEq+ 这类 reference-based equivalence 检查相比，SA-Pass 不要求候选陈述和参考答案在表达形式上接近；与 compile rate 相比，它显式检查语义覆盖和语义不过强/不过弱的问题。","experiments":"实验最有信息量的是三组。第一组是 benchmark 本身。ShadowBench 包含 178 道 Lean 4 full-autoformalization 题，覆盖 8 个数学领域和 3 个难度层级，题目来自研究生到研究级别。作者强调其 reference formalization 平均行数约为既有基准中典型短题的 1.6 倍，更偏多结论和复杂结构。第二组是指标有效性。对 6 种 agentic 配置生成的输出，SA-Pass 与专家二元判断的一致率达到 98.8%，而 compile rate 只有 17.8%，BEq+ 为 80.6%。这里 compile rate 的低一致率不是说编译信息没用，而是它几乎无法区分“编得过但命题错了”的大类错误。第三组是模型结果。Claude Code (Opus 4.8) 配合 Numina-Lean-Agent 在 ShadowBench 上 compile rate 61.8%，但 SA-Pass 仅 11.2%，两者之间有 50 个百分点以上的落差，直接说明当前系统在 statement alignment 上远弱于表面 compile 成绩。论文还分析了常见失败模式，包括只证明了部分结论、把存在性写成全称、遗漏 side conditions、把等价命题写成单向蕴含等。对 benchmark 设计，作者还给出早期 126 题版本在 ICML 2026 AI4Math Challenge Track 4 中的使用情况，说明这个评测不是纸面构造，而是已经进入外部竞赛环境。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction、Section 3 的 SA-Pass 定义、Section 4.1 的 shadow annotation 流程，再看 Table 3 和 Table 4。这样能很快抓住问题、方法和结果。若想判断这个指标能否迁移到自己的 formal reasoning pipeline，重点看两处。第一，看 complete shadow set 的定义和 checker 编译方式，确认它到底在验证什么，尤其是 backward checker 如何避免 shadows 只覆盖局部语义。第二，看 Appendix K 里的 checker patterns，那里更接近实际工程模板，能看出哪些命题结构容易拆成 shadows。阅读时间大约 30–45 分钟。若只关心结论，Table 4 足够；若关心能否拿来做 reward/model selection，则必须细看 annotation cost、失败案例和附录中的 checker 例子。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a precise evaluation bottleneck in autoformalization: how to automatically tell whether a generated Lean theorem statement is semantically aligned with the original informal mathematics. Existing proxies are weak in opposite ways. Compile-based metrics accept many type-correct but semantically wrong statements, while reference-equivalence metrics reject valid formalizations that use a different but equivalent formulation. SA-Pass reframes semantic alignment as a pair of implication checks against a reusable set of shadow theorems. A candidate statement must imply every shadow statement, and the conjunction of shadows must imply the intended theorem. This shifts evaluation from matching one reference form to certifying the semantic content of the theorem.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for the training/evaluation interface in formal reasoning. Its contribution is mainly infrastructural. First, it upgrades statement evaluation from a noisy compile proxy to something close to expert judgment, which directly affects model ranking and any downstream use of automatic signals. Second, it turns one-time expert effort into reusable checkers, making repeated evaluation of models, agents, and decoding strategies much cheaper. Third, it exposes a large gap between syntactic success and semantic correctness. On ShadowBench, Claude Code (Opus 4.8) with Numina-Lean-Agent reaches 61.8% compile rate but only 11.2% SA-Pass. That gap is the main practical message: if future work uses compile success as a reward, filter, or self-training target, it will admit many semantically incorrect positives.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction, then read the SA-Pass definition section and the annotation workflow section, followed by Tables 3 and 4. Second, inspect the checker patterns in the appendix, because they show what kinds of theorem structures can be decomposed cleanly into shadows in practice. advice-seeking requests has a stable structure, rather than treating it as prompt noise. The scope is first-turn advice-seeking conversations where a user asks the model to help choose, recommend, or decide something for their own situation. The central question is whether articulation features such as specificity, usable constraints, named alternatives, hedging, and contextual density form low-dimensional factors separable from topic, and whether those factors are associated with model responses.","importance":"This is not a pretraining algorithm paper, but it is useful for chat data analysis, instruction-data construction, and evaluation design. The main signal is that topic- or task-stratified benchmarks can miss a real input-distribution axis: prompts that are long but poorly specified. The paper estimates that this population is roughly one sixth of advice-seeking traffic. Models tend to answer these prompts with shorter, vaguer responses and few clarifying questions. For post-training and evaluation, this suggests that datasets dominated by well-articulated requests can overestimate real user-facing helpfulness.","technical_approach":"The method is designed as a measurement pipeline, not as a new model. The authors avoid asking an LLM to directly score articulation, because such a score would impose a predefined label and would not reveal how many dimensions the variation has or whether the structure replicates. Instead, the LLM is used for local span extraction, after which interpretable features are fed into unsupervised statistical analysis to recover latent articulation factors. The data comes from English first-turn conversations in WildChat-4.8M, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and ShareChat. A two-pass cascade identifies advice-seeking prompts, producing 16,447 prompts with corresponding assistant replies. A key design choice is asymmetric corpus use: the structure is learned only on WildChat and then applied to LMSYS and ShareChat, so cross-corpus replication is a real test rather than refitting.","experiments":"The main findings are distributional. First, articulation variation in natural chat has a stable, low-dimensional latent structure that replicates across train/test splits and, for the core factors, across corpora. Second, this structure is largely separable from topic, so it is not just another topic cluster. Third, the recovered factors expose a risk population: prompts that are verbose but information-poor, with diffuse asks and weak actionable constraints. This group is estimated at about one in six advice-seeking prompts. Fourth, model behavior covaries with this population: responses are shorter, less specific, include fewer concrete recommendations, and rarely ask clarifying questions. The paper also reports that a single feature can detect the at-risk prompts with AUC=0.91, while arguing that per-prompt scoring cannot replace population-level measurement of dimensionality, reproducibility, and prevalence.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. operator，分成 4 类文本、7 类视觉、3 类音频，并支持单模态、双模态、三模态联合 corruption。为了避免随机扰动带来的偶然性，带随机性的 operator 最多取 3 个随机变体，主指标用均值，同时记录 worst-variant gap 作为 seed-level fragility 的诊断。数据集方面，作者从 Social-IQ、OmniBench、VALOR 的 300 line，意味着这两个通道之间的耦合比单独看任一模态更脆弱。再次，跨模态退化不是简单可加的：双模态或三模态损伤带来的性能下降，不等于单模态下降的线性叠加，而且不同模型的模式不同，说明它们依赖的证据路径并不一样。文中一个醒目的现象是，在固定重度视觉噪声下，14/15 个模型在轻度文本 corruption（t30）下的准确率反而低于重度文本 Firstly, we propose LP","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-04T08:22:50Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2608_28630.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.SD"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 spoken dialogue system 的实时轮次控制问题：系统既要能在用户讲话时插入 backchannel、打断或主动发言，又不能牺牲底层语音大模型的回答质量。难点在于 turn timing 依赖语义、韵律、停顿、重叠语音和交互风格，不能只靠文本轮次边界判断。","importance":"对语音 LM 和统一多模态预训练的启发主要在交互建模。当前很多 speech LLM 仍接近半双工：用户说完，模型再回答。真实对话需要模型同时监听和说话，并学习何时保持沉默、何时短反馈、何时打断。论文的价值不在大规模预训练 recipe，而在把实时 turn-taking 抽象成可插拔控制器、动作空间和数据标注问题。这对构建 speech-native assistant 有参考意义，尤其是把语音流中的非文本信号纳入模型控制。","technical_approach":"核心组件是 LPS-TC，一个轻量级 proactive speech turn controller。它以自回归方式处理用户和助手的双通道音频流，用来捕捉文本模型容易丢失的副语言线索和对话动态。它不是替代 speech LLM，而是作为 plug-and-play 控制器接在半双工或全双工模型外部：对半双工模型，它提供 full-duplex 行为；对已有全双工模型，它改善时机控制。动作空间包含 Normal Turn Taking、Interruptive Turn Taking、Backchanneling、Barge In 和 No Action。Normal Turn Taking 和 Backchanneling 又进一步细分为风格标签，用停顿时长、重叠时长和动作频率等统计量定义。数据方面，论文构建 WildTurn，一个来自真实交互的英文数据集，并用 span-based labeling 标注从触发线索到响应开始的时间区间。它还使用 GPT-5.2 生成 style-conditioned instructions，使控制器可以按不同交互风格工作。评测方面，论文指出静态预录对话会产生上下文错配，因为多轮中后续用户话语受真实助手回复影响。因此它把多轮测试拆成带真实前文的单个 turn，同时设计 chunk-level timing precision 和 turn-level interaction quality 两层评估。","experiments":"论文评测了多类半双工和全双工基线，并把先进半双工 speech LLM 也纳入比较，通过标准 proactive prompting 作为控制基线。结果显示，LPS-TC 在基于其标签空间的 chunk-level timing accuracy 上优于其他 turn controller；接入半双工和全双工 speech LLM 后，也提升 turn-level interaction quality。论文还报告了更好的 style controllability 和鲁棒性。给定内容没有提供具体数值，因此更适合作为方法和评测协议参考，而不是用来判断 scaling 或训练效率。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20 分钟。重点看三部分：LPS-TC 架构图和双通道自回归输入设计，WildTurn 的动作标签和 span 标注方案，以及两层评测协议。若关注 speech-native LM，可以多看动作空间如何把 backchannel、打断和正常接话拆开；若关注数据建设，可以看真实交互数据如何从连续音频流转成训练标签。实验部分主要看不同基座模型接入控制器前后的差异，不必过度关注单一任务分数。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses real-time turn control for spoken dialogue systems. 的框架刻意保持生成端不变，只改“从一批候选里选谁继续往下搜”。设当前 suffix 为 x_t，标准梯度式 proposer 先生成一个候选批次 A_t。传统做法是计算每个候选在训练行为集上的当前攻击 loss，然后直接选 loss 最低者。作者认为这一步过于短视，于是把选择分数改成由三类信号组成。\n\n第一类是当前步质量信号 L_now，也就是常规的 target-token cross-entropy，衡量这个候选此刻对目标有多“顺手”。这部分保留，是因为完全丢掉当前代理目标也不合理。\n\n第二类是轨迹感知信号。正文里给了两个具体构件：一个是 early-token loss，记作 L_first 或类似符号，只看目标响应开头若干 token 的损失；作者的动机是 jailbreak 往往先在回答开头重新出现 refusal 或安全模板，因此前缀是否已经形成“越狱脚手架”比整段平均 loss 更早暴露问题。另一个是 one-step improvement proxy，记作 Δ1(a)，用来估计某个候选是否还能为下一步优化留下空间，或者说它对后续轨迹是否友好。虽然从给出的正文片段看不到全部公式细节，但方法思想很清楚：不要只看候选当前站在哪个点，还要看它把搜索带向什么方向。\n\n第三类是 reference regularization。作者借用了 ORPO 一类工作的思路，在候选选择时引入参考分布或参考候选的约束，避免 selector 过度追逐某个不稳定的局部 proxy。这里的作用不是做额外对齐，而是稳定选择边界，减少因为当前 batch 噪声或代理缺陷导致的激进跳转。\n\n一个值得注意的设计是，TACS 不要求额外查询中间输出，也不访问真实 reward，只使用标准搜索过程中本来就能拿到的信号。这让它更像“改决策规则”而不是“加新监督器”。与现有 jailbreak 优化方法相比，它的创新点不在 proposer，而在 selector；与一般 beam search 或 reranking 不同，它优化的是跨步轨迹质量，而不是单步分数排序。","experiments":"实验核心结论是：在相同搜索预算下，只改候选选择规则，平均 ASR 在 HarmBench 上提升接近 10%。作者把这作为“选择阶段 reward hacking”存在且可修复的主要证据。\n\n从正文描述看，最重要的现象实验是 loss–ASR mismatch：优化过程中训练 loss 平稳下降，但攻击成功率长时间不动，只在后期不规则跳升。这说明当前步 loss 不是一个好的单步保留准则。TACS 通过引入 trajectory-aware 和 reference-regularized 评分后，优化轨迹更稳定，ASR 提升也更持续，而不是偶然撞到有效 suffix。\n\n虽然给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字和全部基线名称，但可以确认作者在 HarmBench 上做了与强基线的同预算比较，并强调“same candidate batch, same observable signals, different selector”这一控制变量。若正文完整实验部分延续这个设置，那么最值得看的应是三类结果：总体 ASR 提升、不同模型/任务上的稳定性、以及各评分项的消融。尤其是 early-token loss 和 Δ1 代理各自贡献多少，决定了这个方法更像是‘前缀敏感选择’还是更一般的‘轨迹价值估计’。","reading_guide":"这篇适合带着问题去泛读。先看 Introduction 里的 Figure 1 或对应轨迹图，确认作者提出的问题是否成立：loss 下降但 ASR 不跟，这个现象如果稳，就说明 selector 确实是瓶颈。\n\n然后重点看 Methodology 里候选评分函数的定义。阅读时建议把三个部分分开理解：L_now 负责当前可行性，early-token loss 负责前缀越狱信号，Δ1 和 reference regularization 负责抑制短视。不要只记公式，要看每一项为什么会改善“当前 proxy 与未来成功率不一致”的问题。\n\n实验部分优先看主表和消融。主表回答它是否在同预算下稳定提升 ASR；消融回答提升来自哪一项。若作者提供了轨迹可视化或 case study，也值得看，因为这类方法的说服力很大程度来自优化路径而不是终点分数。\n\n预计 20 到 30 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。若你做 RLHF、偏好优化、搜索式数据合成或红队攻击，这篇的价值在于它把 reward hacking 具体化到了 selector 这一层。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper does not mainly ask how to generate stronger jailbreak suffix candidates. Instead, it focuses on a more hidden optimization issue: in gradient-based suffix jailbreak search, the retained candidate is usually the one with the lowest current-step loss. The authors show that this rule is myopic. Training loss can decrease steadily while attack success rate stays flat for a long time, which means the selector is over-optimizing a short-horizon proxy and effectively reward-hacking the search process. TACS aims to improve candidate retention under the same search budget and without extra supervision, by favoring candidates that are more useful for the future trajectory rather than just the current step.","importance":"$21","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"$23","reading_guide":"$24","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"指出了越狱攻击优化中的短视问题，其引入的轨迹感知和正则化方法对理解 LLM 安全对齐和对抗训练有一定启发。","en":"Points out the myopia issue in jailbreak attack optimization; its introduced trajectory-aware and regularization methods offer some inspiration for understanding LLM safety alignment and adversarial training."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Affiliation:"],"authors":["Shiliang Xiao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["jailbreak","suffix optimization","reward hacking","safety alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29564","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29564","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.30005","title":"Small Language Models as Judges for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning","abstract":"Rubric-based reinforcement learning extends RL beyond tasks with exact answers or rule-based verifiers by scoring responses against instance-specific criteria. impressive","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-30T03:32:13Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_29530.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"该工作研究一个机制解释问题：连续向量表示的神经网络为何能在语言、逻辑、算术、代码等传统上依赖符号结构的任务上表现良好。","importance":"这项工作对基座模型研究的价值主要在机制分析。它提供了一种可检验的假设：Transformer 和其他网络并不是绕开符号结构，而是在隐藏表示中隐式形成了类似 role–filler 绑定的结构。这个结果如果稳定成立，会影响对组合泛化、ICL、变量绑定、语法和程序表示的理解。它不直接给出新的训练 recipe，但能帮助判断哪些能力可能来自表示几何中的结构化绑定，而不是单纯的模式匹配。","technical_approach":"方法是 DISCOVER，目标是用显式符号结构去近似网络内部向量表示。它采用 Tensor Product Representations, TPR，来表达 role–filler 绑定。filler 是被表示的内容，例如 cats、chase、dogs；role 是位置或功能，例如 subject、verb、object。一个结构由多组 role–filler pair 组成，再映射为向量。作者的关键实验不是只训练一个 probe，而是尝试用一个可解释的 TPR 方程替换网络生成表示的过程，并检查替换后模型行为是否保持。更强的一步是表示干预：例如把 clever 从“主语形容词”位置移动到“宾语形容词”位置，模型后续行为会随之改变，像输入变成了 the doctor helped the clever lawyer。这个因果干预比线性可读出更有说服力，因为它检验表示是否参与了模型计算。","experiments":"实验分两层。第一层是合成序列操作任务，覆盖 MLP、RNN、Transformer 三类网络，结果显示不同架构都能出现可由 TPR 近似的符号结构。第二层是大语言模型分析，包含 Gemma-3-27B、GPT-2-XL、GPT-OSS-20B、Pythia-12B、Qwen3-14B、OLMo-2-13B、Llama-3.1-8B。作者报告这些模型内部都能找到类似结构。对 GPT-OSS 的深入分析覆盖算术、逻辑、代码、语言四类符号任务。实验还展示了 role–filler 的组合泛化：DISCOVER 在训练时见过 scientist 这个 filler，但没有见过它作为 subject，测试时仍能推广到 scientist-as-subject 的组合。这一点比简单分类 probe 更重要，因为它检验结构是否具有系统组合性。","reading_guide":"泛读 30–45 分钟。建议先看 TPR 形式化部分，确认 role、filler、binding、unbinding 如何定义；再看 DISCOVER 如何拟合网络表示，以及替换表示后如何评估行为保持；最后看干预实验和未见 role–filler 组合泛化实验。若时间有限，优先读 GPT-OSS 的四类任务分析和表示编辑案例。对基座预训练研究员来说，最值得深挖的是两个问题：这些结构在训练过程中何时出现；以及不同数据规模、模型规模、架构和目标函数是否会改变 role–filler 绑定的清晰度。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks how neural networks with continuous vector representations can solve tasks that have traditionally been described in symbolic terms, such as language, logic, arithmetic, and code.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model research is mechanistic. It tests the hypothesis that neural networks do not avoid symbolic structure; instead, they may implement it implicitly inside hidden states through role–filler binding. If the finding is robust, it matters for how we reason about compositional generalization, in-context learning, variable binding, syntax, and program-like representations. It does not propose a new pretraining recipe, but it gives a concrete representational account that can be tested during training and across scales.","technical_approach":"The analysis method is DISCOVER, which tries to approximate neural representations with explicitly structured symbolic vectors. The formalism is Tensor Product Representations. A filler is the content being represented, such as cats, chase, or dogs. A role is the position or function, such as subject, verb, or object. A symbolic structure is represented as a set of role–filler pairs and then mapped into a vector. The important step is not just training a probe. The authors try to replace a network’s representation-generating process with a single interpretable TPR-based equation and test whether the model’s behavior changes. They also perform causal edits. For example, moving the representation of clever from a subject-adjective role to an object-adjective role makes the model behave as if the adjective modified the object. This is stronger evidence than linear decodability because it tests whether the structure participates in computation.","experiments":"The experiments cover two regimes. In synthetic sequence-manipulation tasks, the authors analyze MLPs, RNNs, and Transformers and find TPR-like structure across all three architecture classes. In large language models, they analyze Gemma-3-27B, GPT-2-XL, GPT-OSS-20B, Pythia-12B, Qwen3-14B, OLMo-2-13B, and Llama-3.1-8B, and report similar emergent structure. A deeper case study on GPT-OSS covers arithmetic, logic, coding, and language. The paper also tests systematic generalization over role–filler combinations. For instance, DISCOVER can generalize to scientist used as a subject even if that exact role assignment was held out during fitting. This matters because it goes beyond detecting a feature; it tests compositional reuse of content and position.","reading_guide":"A careful skim should take 30–45 minutes. Start with the TPR setup to understand roles, fillers, binding, and unbinding. Then read how DISCOVER fits hidden representations and how the replacement evaluation is performed. Finally, focus on the causal intervention experiments and the held-out role–filler generalization tests. If time is limited, prioritize the GPT-OSS analyses across arithmetic, logic, coding, and language, plus the representation-editing examples. For pretraining research, the most useful follow-up questions are when these structures emerge during training and how they vary with data scale, model scale, architecture, and objective.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"探讨神经网络内部表示是否隐式包含符号结构，对理解 LLM 的泛化和推理机制有启发，值得一读。","en":"Explores whether internal representations of neural networks implicitly contain symbolic structures, offering insights into LLM generalization and reasoning mechanisms; worth skimming."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Yale University","Johns Hopkins University","New York University","Microsoft Research"],"authors":["R. 共享策略、以及“高资源语言是否充当结构锚点”的理解。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者采用 Distributed Alignment Search（DAS）来定位因果相关的内部表示，而不是用 probe 或 SAE/CLT 一类分解方法。原因很明确：probe 过于灵活，容易读出并不存在于原模型决策中的信息；SAE/CLT 虽然能给出“特征”，但稳定性和 faithful causal attribution 仍有争议。DAS 的做法是，在一对最小对比样本之间，对某一层某些 token 位置的表示做受限旋转和替换，寻找能最大程度解释行为翻转的低维子空间。作者先在单语言内做 localization，确认某个 construction 的关键位置和层，再把在源语言学到的干预子空间直接迁移到目标语言对应位置，测量行为是否按预期改变。\n\n数据设计是这篇工作的关键。作者为三类句法现象分别构造模板化最小对，覆盖 15 种语言，但由于类型学差异，并不是每种语言都能覆盖所有现象。例如中文没有语法数，因此不参与主谓数一致；德语和荷兰语因嵌入从句动词后置，filler–gap 模板不能与其他语言完全对齐，所以相关分析做了限制。作者强调模板对齐和最小对控制，目的是让跨语言干预尽量只测试目标句法变量，而不是被词序、词形变化或词汇共现牵着走。\n\n为了避免把“任何干预都能起作用”误判为共享机制，文中设计了三类控制。摘要里明确提到 Random Labels，用来检验干预是否真的针对语法标签而非数据偶然结构；全文还设置了位置或表示层面的对照，用来排除非特异性的表示污染。评价上，作者先做行为层验证，确认模型本身在这些模板上确实掌握了相应句法现象；然后比较语言内干预、跨语言迁移干预、以及控制条件下的效果差异。更进一步，作者把跨语言迁移强度与语言类型学距离联系起来，发现迁移不是随机分布，而是围绕 typological hubs 组织：某些语言因为与更多语言共享结构特征，其内部机制更容易迁移到其他语言。","experiments":"实验覆盖 4 个多语 LM、15 种语言、3 类句法现象。模型横跨不同家族、规模和训练设定，目的是避免结论只成立于某一类 checkpoint。作者先在行为层确认模型对模板任务有足够准确率，否则后续机制分析没有意义。随后在每个语言-现象组合上做 DAS 定位，再进行跨语言 transfer intervention。\n\n主要结果有三层。第一，跨语言机制迁移在三个句法现象上都能稳定观察到，不是只在最接近的语言对里成立。第二，这种迁移是 graded 的，不是“能迁就完全一样，不能迁就完全无关”，而是随语言间类型学接近程度连续变化。第三，存在 hub-like 语言：一些语言中定位到的机制，对更多目标语言都更容易迁移，说明多语模型内部可能形成了围绕若干结构中心组织的句法抽象。\n\n从消融和控制看，随机标签或不匹配干预会显著削弱效果，说明作者测到的不是一般性的表示扰动，而是与目标句法行为相关的因果子空间。文中还比较了不同模型和不同构式，结论的稳健性比单一案例研究更强。需要注意的是，这篇工作更重机制证据而不是追求大表格性能，因此不会给出类似“提升多少 benchmark 分数”的结果；真正重要的是干预后行为翻转幅度、跨语言迁移成功率，以及这些量与类型学关系的对应。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看 Introduction 和 Cross-Lingual LM Interpretability，弄清作者为什么不用 probing、为什么选 DAS。第二，看 Methods 里三类句法模板和跨语言对齐方式，这决定了实验是否可信。第三，看结果部分里关于 transfer strength 与 typology 的分析，这是全文最有信息量的地方。\n\n如果时间有限，建议优先看：Figure 1 把任务设定讲得很直观；模板示例表能帮助理解每个 construction 到底在测什么；主结果图表里比较 within-language intervention、cross-language transfer 和 control 的部分最关键。预计 30–45 分钟可以把主线读清。若要深挖，值得看附录中的模板细节和行为验证，因为这类机制解释工作很容易被数据构造质量决定上限。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a mechanism-level question about multilingual LMs: when they process analogous syntactic phenomena in different languages, do they rely on genuinely shared internal machinery, or do they solve each language separately with only superficial representational overlap? The authors study three controlled constructions—subject–verb number agreement, anaphoric pronoun gender agreement, and filler–gap object extraction—and move beyond probing-style evidence. The key challenge is to first localize causally relevant internal subspaces within each language, then test whether the same intervention transfers across languages in a behaviorally meaningful way.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is interpretive rather than recipe-driven. Much of the multilingual pretraining discussion has relied on probes, alignment metrics, or transfer performance, which can show shared information but not shared computation. This work pushes the question to the causal level. Across four multilingual LMs, fifteen languages, and three syntactic phenomena, it finds evidence that models reuse abstract syntactic mechanisms across languages, and that this reuse is structured by typological similarity rather than being uniform. For people working on multilingual pretraining, this matters because it suggests that parameter sharing is not only a lexical or semantic effect. Mid-level syntactic abstractions also appear to be shared, which has implications for language mixture design, tokenizer sharing, and the role of high-resource languages as structural anchors during training.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"For a quick pass, focus on three parts. First, read the motivation for using causal abstraction and DAS instead of probes. Second, inspect the template construction and cross-lingual alignment details in the methods; the credibility of the whole paper depends on them. Third, spend time on the results connecting transfer strength to typology, because that is where the paper moves from a local interpretability result to a broader claim about multilingual organization.\n\nIf reading selectively, start with Figure 1 for the intervention setup, then the template examples, then the main transfer-result figures comparing within-language localization, cross-language transfer, and controls. A 30–45 minute skim is enough to get the main argument. If you want to go deeper, the appendices on template design and behavioral validation are worth reading, since this kind of mechanistic work is highly sensitive to stimulus quality.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"多语言预训练的人应看方法和结果图：若干语法机制按类型学共享，能约束 tokenizer 与语料混合的假设。","en":"For multilingual pretraining, read the method and result figures: typology-graded shared syntax mechanisms constrain assumptions about tokenization and data mixing."},"metadata":{"institutions":["The University of Texas at Austin","Leipzig University","ScaDS.AI Dresden/Leipzig","Heidelberg Institute for Theoretical Studies","Affiliation:The University of Texas at Austin","Affiliation:Leipzig University, ScaDS.AI Dresden/Leipzig","Affiliation:Heidelberg Institute for Theoretical Studies"],"authors":["Sasha Boguraev","Toshiki Nakai","Kyle Mahowald","Julius Steuer"],"notable_authors":["Kyle Mahowald"],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","multilingual-lm","syntax","causal-intervention","typology"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28924","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.28924","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.28930","title":"The Hallucination Signal Is a Mean Shift: Why Simple Probes Suffice","abstract":"Hidden-state probes effectively detect LLM hallucinations, but the geometry of the signal remains poorly characterized, driving increasingly complex probe architectures. Across three 7B-scale models and three datasets in a paired-example paradigm, we find the signal overwhelmingly dominated by a single mean-shift component, and removing this direction collapses detection to chance. Shrinkage linear discriminant analysis closes about 73% of the gap between 1D and full-dimensional classifiers, so ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-28T23:01:21Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_28930.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题很具体：LLM 幻觉检测里，hidden-state probe 为什么有效，信号几何到底有多复杂，是否真的需要越来越重的 probe 架构。作者把问题放在一个受控的 paired-example 设定下，试图把“表示里有没有可分信号”和“生成过程带来的分布漂移”分开。核心结论是，幻觉信号主要表现为一个单一的均值偏移方向；很多复杂方法看起来更强，主要不是因为抓住了非线性结构，而是因为在高维协方差估计上做了不同程度的正则化。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发有限，但对表征分析、truthfulness probing、训练动态诊断有参考价值。它给出一个很清楚的负结果式判断：在受控设定里，幻觉信号并不需要复杂分类器来刻画，简单线性 probe 已经接近上界。这个结论有两层意义。第一，后续做 hidden-state 诊断时，应该先问信号几何是否真的复杂，而不是默认上大模型 probe。第二，如果某类行为差异主要是 mean shift，那么更值得研究的是这种 shift 在训练中如何形成、分布在哪些层、与数据或优化信号有什么关系，而不是继续堆检测器结构。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者把每个样本表示成某层 hidden state，二分类标签为 factual / hallucinated。然后从最基础的几何量出发：两类样本的类均值 μ0、μ1，以及它们的差 δ=μ1−μ0。作者先问一个必要性问题：如果把表示中沿 δ 的分量去掉，检测性能还剩多少。结果接近随机，说明这个方向几乎承载了全部可用信号。再问一个充分性问题：只投影到 δ 这一维，能恢复多少性能。结果 1D 投影已经拿到 0.834 AUROC，恢复了大部分可分性，但仍落后于正则化 logistic regression。\n\n这就引出第二层分析：剩余差距来自真正的高阶非线性结构，还是仅仅来自高维协方差估计。作者用 Fisher LDA 的视角解释，最优线性方向应为 ΣW^-1δ，而不是原始 δ；当维度约 4000 且协方差远非球形时，协方差建模会带来额外收益。于是他们比较了 raw mean-shift、shrinkage LDA、L2-regularized logistic regression，以及一组有针对性的替代方法，去区分“均值驱动”“方差驱动”“非线性驱动”三类可能性。结果显示 shrinkage LDA 已经能弥合约 73% 的 1D 与全维分类器之间差距，说明主要难点是高维噪声下的协方差估计，而不是复杂决策边界。\n\n在此基础上，作者提出 LayerMix。它不是一个复杂新模型，而是一个由几何观察直接推出来的工程化策略：既然信号不是集中在单个 oracle layer，而是分布在一段连续层带上，就用交叉验证在多层上做轻量聚合，自动找到信息层带并组合其分数。作者明确把 LayerMix 定位成一个强而简单的 baseline，而不是新的理论贡献。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个 7B 级模型和 3 个数据集，统一采用 paired-example 范式。主结果里，去掉均值偏移方向 δ 后，检测 AUROC 降到 0.499，几乎等于随机猜测，说明 δ 是必要的。只保留 δ 的 1D 投影时，AUROC 为 0.834，已经恢复了约 78% 的高于随机部分。实际可用的 L2 正则 logistic regression 达到 0.952 AUROC，表明在 δ 之外仍有少量可利用结构，但主要是线性、且与协方差形状有关。\n\n作者进一步报告，沿 δ 方向的 Cohen’s d 在 1.2–1.6 之间，尽管这个方向解释的总方差不到 3%。这说明“可分性强”与“解释方差大”不是一回事：一个低方差方向也可以承载主要判别信号。对比实验中，L2-LR 与 12 种受控替代架构比较，整体上持平或更好，支持“复杂 probe 多半是在和估计噪声搏斗”这一判断。LayerMix 在不需要 oracle layer 的情况下达到 0.954 AUROC，与 oracle 设定相当，额外计算开销约 35 秒，说明多层聚合的收益主要来自利用连续层带中的分布式信号，而不是更复杂的建模。\n\n值得注意的是，作者反复强调这些结论受限于 paired-example 控制设定，不应直接外推到所有在线检测或自由生成场景。这种边界意识是加分项，也意味着如果把方法搬到真实生成流水线，性能和几何结构都可能变化。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确。先看 Introduction 和 important question: why do hidden-state probes work so well for hallucination detection, and how complicated is the underlying signal geometry really? Instead of assuming that stronger detection requires increasingly elaborate probe architectures, the authors study the problem in a controlled paired-example setting designed to separate intrinsic representational structure from generation-induced distribution shift. Their central claim is that the hallucination signal is dominated by a single mean-shift direction, and that much of the apparent need for complex models comes from high-dimensional covariance estimation rather than meaningful nonlinearity.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to representation analysis and model diagnostics.
