Specifically, we introduc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-29T14:45:09Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_29305.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"该工作解决 LLM Web Agent 在复杂真实网页中组合泛化失败的问题：单个页面同时包含多个功能区域，模型难以从冗余观察中定位与当前决策相关的局部上下文。","importance":"对预训练方向的直接贡献有限，但对 post-train 与 test-time adaptation 有参考价值。它把失败原因从“任务太长、奖励太稀疏”进一步拆到“观察空间没有被结构化”。这对训练数据构造、网页轨迹合成、agent RL reward 设计都有启发：与其直接在完整网页上学策略，不如让模型先学会把环境分解成可操作的局部决策单元。","technical_approach":"方法叫 TTED，即 Test-Time Environment Decomposition。核心做法是在正式执行前加入 trial step，让 agent 将复杂网页观察拆成多个 sub-module，每个模块由子目标和相关子观察组成。之后 agent 不直接在完整页面上更新行为，而是在这些局部环境中利用交互经验做适配。单轮、静态网页场景下，TTED 用 in-context learning 保存试探经验；多轮真实网页场景下，TTED 用 RL 跨步骤、跨任务收集经验。反馈信号主要来自模型自评，而不是人工标签或真实环境奖励。这个选择有现实意义：真实 WebArena 类任务的 ground-truth reward 稀缺且延迟，局部自评虽然有噪声，但在分解后的环境中更容易稳定。","experiments":"实验覆盖合成和真实网页任务。作者扩展了 CompWoB，构造 CompWoB+，用于系统控制环境组合复杂度。结果显示，当页面组合复杂度上升时，普通 agent 的探索成功率和自评准确率都会下降；TTED-ICL 能缓解这种退化。真实环境上，TTED-RL 在 WebArena 上提升泛化表现，并报告超过使用 ground-truth reward 训练的 RL 版本。该工作还分析了 self-assessment 质量、在 WorkArena 上的分布外泛化、数据规模与环境规模影响，以及 RL 算法设计。摘要和引言未给出关键表格中的具体数值，精确提升幅度需要看实验表。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看三部分：第一，Figure 1 对失败模式的拆解，尤其是探索成功率和自评准确率如何随环境复杂度下降；第二，TTED 的环境分解定义和 trial step 设计，看它如何把完整 MDP 转成局部决策上下文；第三，WebArena 和 WorkArena 实验，重点比较 TTED-RL、普通 test-time learning、ground-truth reward RL 的差异。对预训练研究员而言，最有用的是它对“可学习环境结构”的建模，而不是网页任务本身。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work addresses a failure mode of LLM web agents in realistic web environments: a single observation contains many functional regions, and the agent fails to isolate the local context that matters for the next decision.","importance":"Its direct contribution to base pretraining is limited, but it is useful for thinking about the pretrain–posttrain interface for agents. The key signal is that web-agent failures are not only caused by long horizons or sparse rewards. They also come from unstructured observations. This suggests a training recipe where models learn to decompose environments into local decision units before learning full trajectories.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is TTED, Test-Time Environment Decomposition. It inserts a trial step before normal execution. During this step, the agent partitions a complex web observation into sub-modules, each with a sub-goal and a task-relevant sub-observation. The agent then adapts its behavior inside these local contexts rather than optimizing directly in the full page. For single-turn static pages, TTED uses in-context learning to reuse trial experience. For multi-step realistic web tasks, it uses reinforcement learning over collected interaction experience. The feedback is mainly model self-assessment, not human labels or ground-truth rewards. This design is motivated by the fact that real web rewards are sparse, delayed, and expensive, while local decomposition makes self-assessment less brittle.","experiments":"The evaluation covers both synthetic and realistic web tasks. The authors extend CompWoB into CompWoB+ to control and scale environment composition complexity. They report that standard agents suffer lower exploration success and weaker self-assessment as composition complexity grows, while TTED with ICL reduces this degradation. On realistic WebArena tasks, TTED with RL improves generalization and is reported to outperform RL trained with ground-truth rewards. The paper also analyzes self-assessment quality, out-of-distribution transfer to WorkArena, data and environment scaling, and RL algorithm choices. It aims to turn repeated evidence gathering into cheaper structured lookups, trading off the high upfront cost of full pre-structuring against the high online token cost of raw document reading.","experiments":"The abstract reports that, on FanOutQA, an ideal pre-structured store can make reasoning 28x cheaper, and that the gap grows by orders of magnitude as questions fan out over more documents.
